  (fea_conv): Conv2d(3, 128, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
  (post_unet_esa): ESA(
    (conv1): Conv2d(128, 32, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
    (conv2): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
    (conv3): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
    (conv_f): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
    (conv4): Conv2d(32, 128, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
    (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
    (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
  )
  (post_unet_conv): Conv2d(128, 128, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
  (B1): P_HTCB(
    (tesa_in): TESA(
      (esa1): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(128, 32, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv_f): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(32, 128, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
      (esa2): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(128, 32, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv_f): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(32, 128, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
      (esa3): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(128, 32, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv_f): Conv2d(32, 32, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(32, 128, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
    )
    (tcn1): TCN(
      (hat): HAT(
        (hat_group): HAT_Group(
          (blocks): ModuleList(
            (0-3): 4 x HAB(
              (norm1): LayerNorm((128,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
              (attn): WindowAttention(
                (qkv): Linear(in_features=128, out_features=384, bias=True)
                (attn_drop): Dropout(p=0.0, inplace=False)
                (proj): Linear(in_features=128, out_features=128, bias=True)
                (proj_drop): Dropout(p=0.0, inplace=False)
                (softmax): Softmax(dim=-1)
              )
              (drop_path): Identity()
              (norm2): LayerNorm((128,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
              (mlp): Mlp(
                (fc1): Linear(in_features=128, out_features=256, bias=True)
                (act): GELU(approximate='none')
                (fc2): Linear(in_features=256, out_features=128, bias=True)
                (drop): Dropout(p=0.0, inplace=False)
              )
              (conv_du): Sequential(
                (0): Conv2d(128, 32, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
                (1): ReLU(inplace=True)
                (2): Conv2d(32, 128, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
                (3): Sigmoid()
              )
            )
          )
          (ocab): OCAB(
            (norm1): LayerNorm((128,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
            (attn): WindowAttention(
              (qkv): Linear(in_features=128, out_features=384, bias=True)
              (attn_drop): Dropout(p=0.0, inplace=False)
              (proj): Linear(in_features=128, out_features=128, bias=True)
              (proj_drop): Dropout(p=0.0, inplace=False)
              (softmax): Softmax(dim=-1)
            )
            (drop_path): Identity()
            (norm2): LayerNorm((128,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
            (mlp): Mlp(
              (fc1): Linear(in_features=128, out_features=256, bias=True)
